Motivation: 研究椭圆曲线L-级数泰勒展开系数的非零性，是数论中关于L-函数特殊值及算术信息的重要问题，尤其是二次扭转族中的系数行为。

Method: 利用广义黎曼假设(GRH)分析系数非零性，并运用模L-函数二次扭转导数中心值的矩理论推导无条件下界。

Result: 在GRH下，当d足够大时二次扭转族中系数非零；无条件情况下，得到非零系数数量的下界。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对椭圆曲线L-级数系数分布的理解，并为二次扭转族中算术不变量提供了理论支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the coefficients of the Taylor expansion of the complex $L$-series of any elliptic curve over $\mathbb{Q}$. We prove that, in the family of quadratic twists by all the discriminants $d$, these coefficients are nonvanishing under GRH when $d$ is sufficiently large. Unconditionally, we obtain a general lower bound for the number of nonvanishing coefficients in the family of quadratic twists, through a series of results from the moments of the central values of the derivatives of quadratic twists of modular $L$-function.

</details>


### [93] [Low-Lying Zeros on the Critical Line for Families of Dirichlet $L$-Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09282)
*XinHang Ji*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对Dirichlet L-函数在极短区间内临界线上低零点的个数建立了新的下界。对充分大的素数P和特定范围的实数T，证明了特征和∑ N₀(T, χ) ≫ T²P√(log P)。通过引入高维Mellin变换的新分析框架，克服了Levinson方法和标准Selberg磨光器在短区间上的技术障碍，成功处理了复杂的交叉项问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法（Levinson方法和Selberg磨光器）在处理极短区间内L-函数零点分布时遇到固有技术瓶颈：Levinson方法自身存在局限性无法处理如此受限的区间，而Selberg磨光器的标准应用会因难以处理的不可分离交叉项而受阻。因此需要发展新方法来解决短区间瓶颈问题。

Method: 引入基于高维Mellin变换的新型分析框架，系统性地处理磨光器计算中产生的多变量级数，显式地解决交叉项障碍，从而提取局部化下界。

Result: 对充分大的素数P和T ∈ [a₁/√(log P), 1]，证明了∑_{χ mod P} N₀(T, χ) ≫ T²P√(log P)。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于高维Mellin变换的方法成功绕过了短区间瓶颈问题，不仅建立了新的下界，还对更高秩L-函数族的零点统计具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a new lower bound for the number of low-lying zeros of Dirichlet $L$-functions $L(s, χ)$ on the critical line within extremely short intervals. Specifically, for a sufficiently large prime $P$ and real number $T \in [a_1/\sqrt{\log P}, 1]$, we prove that the sum of the number of zeros on the critical line $N_0(T, χ)$ over characters $χ\bmod P$ satisfies $$ \sum_{χ\bmod P} N_0(T, χ) \gg T^2 P\sqrt{\log P} .$$ Traditional approaches encounter significant technical barriers in this short-interval regime. The Levinson method fails due to its own inherent limitations in handling such restricted intervals , while standard applications of the Selberg mollifier are hindered by the emergence of complex, inseparable cross-terms that are difficult to evaluate. To overcome these obstacles, we introduce a novel analytic framework utilizing high-dimensional Mellin transforms. This approach systematically manages the multi-variable series generated by the mollifier calculations. By explicitly resolving these cross-term obstructions, we extract the localized lower bound, providing a robust method that circumvents the short-interval bottleneck and offers potential applicability to the zero statistics of higher-rank $L$-function families.

</details>

Method: 通过解析数论方法，结合自守形式的谱理论和对模数q施加无平方因子及小素因子限制的条件，推导出指数分布的上界。

Abstract: Let $(λ_f(n))_{n\geqslant1}$ be the Hecke eigenvalues of a holomorphic cusp form $f$. We prove that the exponent of distribution of $λ_f*1$ in arithmetic progressions is as large as $\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{70}$ when the modulus $q$ is square-free and has only sufficiently small prime factors.

</details>


### [95] [An analogue of a formula of Popov II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09607)
*Pedro Ribeiro*

Method: 将原公式中的第一类贝塞尔函数替换为更一般的Whittaker函数，并采用与原始证明截然不同的论证方法完成推广公式的证明。

Result: 成功建立了一个推广的求和公式，其中涉及算术函数 r_k(n) 和Whittaker函数。

Conclusion: 该推广公式将原有结果从贝塞尔函数情形拓展至Whittaker函数情形，且新证明方法为后续进一步推广提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Let $r_{k}(n)$ denote the number of representations of the positive integer $n$ as the sum of $k$ squares. We prove a generalization of a summation formula already proved by us [Advances in Applied Mathematics, 175 (2026) 103201], which involves the arithmetical function $r_{k}(n)$ and the Bessel functions of the first kind. We extend the Bessel functions in the aforementioned formula to Whittaker functions, and our proof of this generalization is drastically different from the proof of the particular case presented in [Advances in Applied Mathematics, 175 (2026) 103201].

</details>


### [96] [The variation of zeros of the Miller basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09731)
*Liubomir Chiriac,Andrei Jorza*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Miller基模形式零点变化与对数Szegő曲线之间的联系，分析了不同δ=m/ℓ值下零点的分布位置，给出了当δ<0.6194时零点全部位于单位弧上的证明，以及δ趋近于1时零点位于对数Szegő曲线上的结果，并枚举了ℓ-m≤25的所有代数零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Miller基模形式中零点分布与对数Szegő曲线之间的深层联系，理解参数δ=m/ℓ如何影响零点的几何位置。

Method: 通过建立Miller基模形式q^m+O(q^{ℓ+1})的零点变化与对数Szegő曲线S_δ之间的数学关联，对不同的δ值范围进行渐近分析和分类讨论，并辅以代数零点的枚举验证。

Result: 当δ<0.6194时，对于足够大的k，所有零点均位于单位弧上；当δ渐近趋近于1时，所有零点位于对数Szegő曲线S_δ上；对于一般δ，零点位于单位弧与对数Szegő曲线的并集上（获得部分结果）；给出了m/ℓ的阈值猜想；枚举了ℓ-m≤25的所有代数零点。

Conclusion: Miller基模形式的零点分布由参数δ=m/ℓ决定，存在从单位弧到对数Szegő曲线的转变，且存在明确的阈值区间。

Abstract: We exhibit a connection between the variation of zeros in the Miller basis of modular forms $q^m+O(q^{\ell+1})$ and a logarithmic version $\mathcal{S}_δ$ of the Szegő curve, where $δ=m/\ell$. When $δ<0.6194$ we show that all the zeros are on the unit arc for $k\gg 0$, while if $δ$ is asymptotically close to 1, we show that all the zeros lie on $\mathcal{S}_δ$. In general, we posit that for all $δ$, the zeros are located on the union of the unit arc and the log Szegő curve, obtaining a partial result, and find conjectural thresholds for $m/\ell$ with all zeros on the unit arc, and no zeros on the arc. Finally, we enumerate all algebraic zeros of Miller forms up to $\ell-m\leq 25$.

</details>

Abstract: We study the rational coefficients that arise when the Eisenstein series $G_k$ is expressed as a polynomial in $G_4$ and $G_6$. The proof uses lacunary recurrences for Eisenstein series.

### [98] [On Ramanujan Primes for Hecke-Maass Cusp Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09807)
*Tinghao Huang,Shifan Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Ramanujan猜想对于多个原始Hecke-Maass尖形式同时成立的素数存在性及密度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ramanujan猜想是模形式理论中的核心问题，之前的研究多关注单个尖形式，本文将其推广到多个尖形式同时成立的情形。

Method: 利用解析数论和自守形式的工具，结合素数分布理论，给出同时满足Ramanujan猜想的最小素数的上界，以及满足条件的素数集的下自然密度的下界。

Result: 给出了两个或三个不同的原始Hecke-Maass尖形式同时满足Ramanujan猜想的最小素数的上界，以及任意多个尖形式中至少一个满足Ramanujan猜想的素数集的下自然密度的下界。

Conclusion: 将Ramanujan猜想从单个尖形式推广到多个尖形式的情形，得到了同时性素数存在性和密度方面的定量结果。

Abstract: For a primitive Hecke-Maass cusp form $φ$ of level $N$ with the $n$-th Hecke eigenvalue $λ_φ(n)$ and a prime number $p\nmid N$, the celebrated Ramanujan conjecture at $p$ asserts the following sharp upper bound: \[
  |λ_φ(p)| \leq 2. \] In this work, we determine an upper bound for the least prime $p$ at which the Ramanujan conjecture holds for two or three distinct primitive Hecke-Maass cusp forms simultaneously. Moreover, given a set of distinct primitive Hecke-Maass cusp forms $\{φ_i\}$, we also provide a lower bound for the lower natural density of the set of primes at which the Ramanujan conjecture holds for at least one of the $φ_i$'s.

</details>


### [99] [Congruences of first syntomic cohomology groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09987)
*Yu Min*

Method: 通过比较两个自反F-gauge的第一合成上同调群的模p^n约化，与它们对应的Breuil-Kisin模（带有G_K作用和Nygaard滤过）的模p^{2n}约化，证明两者同构的充要条件。

Result: 两个自反F-gauge的第一合成上同调模p^n约化同构，当且仅当它们对应的带G_K作用和Nygaard滤过的Breuil-Kisin模的模p^{2n}约化同构。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了合成上同调与Breuil-Kisin模在有限模约化下的精确对应关系，为局部Bloch-Kato Selmer群的精细结构提供了新的刻画工具。

Abstract: Let O_K be the ring of integers of a finite extension K of Q_p. Given two reflexive F-gauges on O_K, we show that for large enough n, the mod p^n-reductions of their first syntomic cohomology groups, which might be regarded as a refinement of local Bloch--Kato Selmer groups, are isomorphic if and only if the mod p^{2n}-reductions of their attached Breuil--Kisin modules with G_K-actions and Nygaard filtrations are isomorphic.

</details>


### [100] [The Serre Derivatives and Zeros of Modular Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10227)
*Naoki Sugibayashi*

Method: 通过对弱全纯模形式及其Serre导数的零点分布进行分析，利用标准基本区域中的边界性质进行推导和证明。

Result: 证明了若弱全纯模形式在标准基本区域中的所有零点均位于下边界，则其Serre导数的零点也全部位于下边界。

Abstract: Since the work of F. Rankin and Swinnerton-Dyer on the zeros of Eisenstein series, many results have been obtained concerning the zeros of modular forms. In this paper, we study the zeros of Serre derivatives of modular forms. In particular, we prove that if all the zeros of a weakly holomorphic modular form in the standard fundamental domain lie on the lower boundary, then the same property holds for its Serre derivative.

</details>

Abstract: We give an explicit formula for the Galois descent expressing multiple $t$-values of maximal height in terms of classical multiple zeta values, making precise Murakami's earlier motivic result. Result: 成功证明了Andrews和Bachraoui的猜想，建立了双色划分生成函数与Hecke型双求和之间的等价关系。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了一个未解决的猜想，还进一步展示了不定theta函数和mock theta函数在数论和组合数学中的强大应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove a conjecture of Andrews and Bachraoui relating a generating function arising from two-color partitions (with odd smallest part and restrictions on the even parts) to a Hecke-type double sum. Our proof is based on Zwegers' theory of indefinite theta functions together with modular transformation properties of mock theta functions.

</details>


### [103] [Reciprocals of Subsum Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10512)
*Cristina Ballantine,George Beck,Brooke Feigon,Kathrin Maurischat*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入分拆λ的子集多项式sp(λ,x)=∏(1+x^{λ_i})，研究所有n的分拆的sp(λ,x)倒数之和，证明相关多项式的算术性质并揭示与其他组合对象的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定义新的分拆多项式生成函数，探索其倒数之和的算术性质及与经典组合对象的关联。

Method: 引入子集多项式sp(λ,x)，通过对所有n的分拆求和，研究其倒数之和的生成函数与算术性质。

Result: 证明了该和式所涉及多项式的算术性质，并建立了与其他组合对象的联系。

Conclusion: 子集多项式倒数之和具有深刻的算术结构，并与已有组合对象存在内在关联。

Abstract: We introduce the subsum polynomial of a partition $λ=(λ_1, λ_2, \ldots, λ_k)$ defined by $\mathrm{sp}(λ, x)=\prod_{i=1}^k(1+x^{λ_i})$. We study the sum of reciprocals of $\mathrm{sp}(λ, x)$ over all partitions of $n$. We prove arithmetic properties of related polynomials and offer connections to other combinatorial objects.

</details>


### [104] [$p$-adic Congruencens of Generalized Euler Numbers and Relations to Even Zeta Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10677)
*Yuta Nishibuchi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了同余欧拉数（广义欧拉数的进一步推广），证明了其p-adic同余性质（包含对Lehmer数相关猜想的解答），并通过复分析给出了用同余欧拉数表示偶数zeta值的表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进一步推广广义欧拉数，提出同余欧拉数的概念；解决与Lehmer数相关的猜想；建立同余欧拉数与偶数zeta值之间的新联系。

Method: 定义同余欧拉数作为广义欧拉数的推广；运用p-adic分析方法证明同余性质；利用复分析技术推导偶数zeta值的表达式。

Result: 证明了同余欧拉数的p-adic同余性质，解答了与Lehmer数相关的猜想；得到了偶数zeta值用同余欧拉数表示的闭合表达式。

Conclusion: 同余欧拉数具有重要的p-adic同余性质，可应用于数论中的猜想证明；同时与解析数论中的zeta值建立了桥梁，展现了其在纯数学中的理论价值。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce congruential Euler numbers, which are a further generalization of generalized Euler numbers. We prove the $p$-adic congruences of congruential Euler numbers, which include answers to a conjecture related to Lehmer numbers. We also provide expressions of even zeta values using congruential Euler numbers via complex analysis.

</details>

### [105] [Bound-State Spectra of a Lifshitz-Type Dirac Equation in (2+1) Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09249)
*Lucas K. R. Queiroz,Van Sérgio Alves,Nilberto Bezerra,Luis Fernández,Francisco Peña*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了(2+1)维 Lifshitz 修正的 Dirac 方程（动力学指数 z=2），通过解析和数值方法分析了径向约束下的束缚态能谱，揭示了 Lifshitz 参数 b 主导的标度律，对二维 Dirac 系统中高阶空间导数修正束缚态谱提供了系统性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解 Lifshitz 空间导数（高阶导数）对 Dirac 系统束缚态能谱的影响，为具有二次低能色散的材料（如双层石墨烯）提供有效理论描述。

Method: 对常数背景、硬壁约束和谐振势采用解析求解；对对数约束采用 Numerov 数值方法结合半经典 WKB 分析。

Result: 得到三种约束下的能谱标度律：硬壁约束 E - M ∝ b/R₀²，谐振约束 E - M ∝ √(2b) ω，对数约束半经典区 E - M ~ α ln√b。

Conclusion: 系统刻画了 Lifshitz 参数 b 对二维 Dirac 束缚态谱的修正规律，对双层石墨烯等二次色散材料的有效理论具有参考价值。

Abstract: We investigate a Dirac-type equation in (2+1) dimensions modified by Lifshitz spatial derivatives with dynamical exponent $z=2$, focusing on the spectral properties of bound states under radial confinement. Analytical solutions are obtained for constant backgrounds, hard-wall confinement, and harmonic potentials, while logarithmic confinement is treated numerically via the Numerov method and complemented by a semiclassical WKB analysis. The resulting spectra exhibit characteristic scaling laws governed by the Lifshitz parameter $b$, including $E - M \propto b/R_0^2$ for hard-wall confinement, $E - M \propto \sqrt{2b}\,ω$ for harmonic trapping, and $E - M \sim α\ln\sqrt{b}$ in the semiclassical regime of logarithmic confinement. These results provide a consistent characterization of how higher-order spatial derivatives modify bound-state spectra in two-dimensional Dirac systems and may be relevant for effective descriptions of materials with quadratic low-energy dispersion, such as bilayer graphene and related anisotropic 2D systems.

</details>


### [106] [Microscopic resonant-shell mechanism for slow Liouvillian sectors in an open correlated lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09274)
*X. Z. Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个微观理论，解释开放关联晶格中慢李雅普诺夫扇区的选择机制。通过定义一种复合壳层轨道——由局域双占据子(doublon)与分支分辨的最近邻键共振构成——并利用Schur投影框架导出边缘记忆律、壳层临界双峰、密度修饰哈密顿量及缺陷生成器，揭示了储层工程化的快块如何作为可观测慢扇区的选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中，慢李雅普诺夫扇区如何被选择是一个基础但未解决的问题。现有理论通常从假设的非厄米能带出发，缺乏微观机制。本文旨在从局域相互作用共振出发，建立慢扇区选择的微观理论。

Method: 方法核心是Schur投影框架。首先定义由局域doublon与分支分辨键共振构成的复合壳层轨道，该轨道的doublon权重控制储层可见性，混合doublon-键特征控制壳层迁移率。将微观跳跃投影到所选壳层上，得到分支选择二聚化通道。在稀薄极限下，边界doublon损耗通道通过Zeno型回归产生指数慢的边缘记忆极点；在壳层临界点，边缘极点被代之以代数量子化的近零驻波双峰；在有限壳层填充下，壳层变密度修饰，粒子数守恒的锁相跳变移除亮失配扇区，缺陷成为渐近慢变量并产生扩散有限尺寸能隙。

Result: 导出了局域壳层、投影分支拓扑、边缘记忆律、壳层临界双峰、密度修饰壳层哈密顿量及缺陷生成器。机制表明储层工程化的快块充当可观测慢扇区的选择器，而微观母壳层保持不变。

Conclusion: 该理论统一描述了开放关联晶格中慢扇区的选择机制，揭示了慢动力学来源于局域共振结构而非非厄米能带假设，为理解开放量子多体系统中的慢弛豫现象提供了微观框架。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic theory for how slow Liouvillian sectors are selected in an open correlated lattice. The starting point is not a postulated non-Hermitian band, but a local interacting resonance between an on-site doublon and a branch-resolved nearest-neighbor bond. This resonance defines a composite shell orbital whose doublon weight controls reservoir visibility and whose mixed doublon-bond character controls shell mobility. Projecting the microscopic hopping onto the selected shell yields a branch-selective dimerized channel. In the dilute regime, a boundary doublon-loss channel yields an exponentially slow edge-memory pole through a Zeno-type return. At the shell-critical point, the edge pole is replaced by a near-zero standing-wave doublet with an algebraic coherent spacing. At finite shell filling, the same local shell becomes density dressed. A number-conserving phase-locking jump removes a bright mismatch sector, leaving defects as the asymptotic slow variables and producing a diffusive finite-size gap. We derive the local shell, the projected branch topology, the edge-memory law, the shell-critical doublet, the density-dressed shell Hamiltonian, and the defect generator within one Schur-projection framework. The resulting mechanism identifies the reservoir-engineered fast block as the selector of the observable slow sector, while the microscopic parent shell remains fixed.

</details>


### [107] [Spin Quadrupolar orders in $d$-wave Unconventional Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09499)
*Jian-Keng Yuan,Zhiming Pan,Congjun Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了d波非常规磁性态（交变磁体）中，动量空间的固有自旋四极有序如何通过弱非磁性周期势与线性响应理论转化为实空间交错磁分布，建立了动量空间多极子与实空间自旋多极序之间的物理联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然d波α相非常规磁性态（交变磁体）在动量空间中具有各向异性的d波特征，但其微观拓扑结构如何转化为实空间的自旋分布仍不清楚。

Method: 将弱非磁性周期晶格势引入d波非常规磁性态，利用线性响应理论计算自旋-电荷交叉磁化率。

Result: 晶格势与d波自旋分裂的相互作用自然地诱导出实空间自旋四极分布，且无需扩大晶胞。

Conclusion: 建立了动量空间偶分波道多极子与实空间自旋多极序之间的物理连接。

Abstract: Unconventional magnetism represents a class of metallic states whose Fermi surfaces exhibit spin-dependent splittings under the non-trivial representations of the rotation group. The $d$-wave $α$-phase unconventional magnetic state, commonly known as altermagnet, recently, has attracted significant attention. While these systems exhibit distinct anisotropic $d$-wave characteristics in momentum space, how this microscopic topology translates into the spin distributions in real space remains a question. In this work, we bridge the intrinsic spin quadrupolar ordering in momentum space to the real-space staggered magnetic distribution. By introducing a weak, non-magnetic periodic crystal potential into a $d$-wave unconventional magnetic state, the spin-charge cross susceptibility is calculated by using the linear response theory. We reveal that the interplay between the crystal potential and the intrinsic $d$-wave spin-splitting naturally induces a spatial spin quadrupole distribution without enlarging the unit cell. Our study thus provides a physical connection between momentum-space multipoles in the even partial wave channel and real-space spin multipole orders.

</details>


### [108] [Boundary-dependent topological degeneracy in an Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09510)
*E. S. Ma,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了具有两种开放边界条件的横场Ising链在非零温度下的相图，发现一种替代边界条件（移除两端耦合和横场）可将系统精确映射到两个独立的Kitaev链，导致拓扑Kramers-like简并在相图中发生切换，其机制源于SSH链中缠绕数的规范依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有两种开放边界条件的Ising链在非零温度下的相图，特别关注一种移除两端耦合和横场的替代边界条件，探索其拓扑简并性的变化机制。

Method: 将系统精确映射到两个独立的Kitaev链，其中无自旋费米子对应畴壁激发；利用Majorana表示分析拓扑Kramers-like简并切换的机制；通过有限尺寸系统的数值模拟展示非零温度下的体-边对应关系。

Result: 替代边界条件导致拓扑Kramers-like简并在相图中发生切换；该切换源于SSH链中缠绕数的规范依赖性；在非零温度下通过数值模拟验证了体-边对应关系的表现。

Conclusion: 该发现为量子自旋链的理解提供了新的见解。

Abstract: The topological degeneracy is a characteristic of quantum phase diagram in an Ising chain with transverse field. We revisit the phase diagram at nonzero temperature of an Ising chain with two types of open boundary conditions. In this work, we focus on an alternative boundary condition that not only removes the coupling between the two end sites but also eliminates the transverse field on them. We show that such a system can be exactly mapped onto two independent Kitaev chains, where spinless fermions correspond to domain-wall excitations. This results in a switch in the existence of the topological Kramers-like degeneracy in the phase diagram. The underlying mechanism is analyzed within the Majorana representation, which indicates that such a switch arises from the gauge dependence of the winding number in an SSH chain. The manifestation of bulk-boundary correspondence at nonzero temperature is demonstrated by numerical simulations on finite-size systems. This finding provides insight into the quantum spin chain.

</details>


### [109] [Spin-charge separation in two-leg t-J ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09705)
*Luhang Yang,Elbio Dagotto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过DMRG方法研究两腿t-J梯子模型，发现通过调节plaquette对角跳跃、自旋交换和空穴掺杂等参数，系统可以从Luther-Emery相（自旋能隙、电荷无能隙）过渡到Luttinger液体相（自旋和电荷均无能隙），并出现自旋-电荷分离的特征，为在更高维系统中实现自旋-电荷分离提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-电荷分离是一维费米子系统的标志性特征，但在更高维度中是否能够实现仍然是一个悬而未决的问题。本文旨在探索在准一维梯子系统中实现自旋-电荷分离的可能性。

Method: 利用密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)方法及其含时扩展，研究两腿t-J梯子模型，分析基态关联和单粒子移除谱，系统考察plaquette对角跳跃、自旋交换和空穴掺杂等参数的影响。

Result: 在适当的参数区间内，这些因素驱动系统从Luther-Emery相（自旋有能隙、电荷无能隙）转变为Luttinger液体相（自旋和电荷均无能隙），并出现自旋-电荷分离的特征。

Conclusion: 结合先前使用精确对角化的研究，本文结果为自旋-电荷分离可能存在于更宽的梯子系统中提供了证据。

Abstract: Spin-charge separation is a hallmark of one-dimensional fermionic systems, yet its realization in higher dimensions remains an open question. To address this issue, we investigate a two-leg t-J ladder using the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) method and its time-dependent extension. By analyzing ground-state correlations and single-particle removal spectra, we systematically examine the effects of plaquette diagonal hopping, spin exchange, and hole doping. Within appropriate parameter regimes, these factors drive the system from the well-known Luther Emery phase, with gapped spin and gapless charge modes, into a Luttinger liquid phase characterized by gapless spin and charge excitations, where signatures of spin-charge separation emerge. In combination with previous studies using exact diagonalization, our results provide evidence that spin-charge separation may persist in wider ladder systems.

</details>


### [110] [Non-magnetic insulating phase induced by Jahn-Teller effect in RNiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09713)
*Sangeeta Rajpurohit,Liang Z. Tan,Tadashi Ogitsu,Peter E. Blöchl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对稀土镍酸盐RNiO₃提出了一个三维多轨道紧束缚模型，统一处理电荷、自旋、轨道和晶格自由度。模型参数来自LuNiO₃的杂化泛函DFT计算。研究发现三种竞争绝缘相由U-3J与电子-声子耦合的竞争决定，并预测了一种新型亚稳态非磁电荷有序绝缘相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立统一的微观理论模型，揭示稀土镍酸盐RNiO₃中电荷、自旋、轨道和晶格自由度之间的复杂相互作用机制，特别是金属-绝缘体转变的物理起源。

Method: 构建三维多轨道紧束缚模型，从杂化泛函DFT计算中提取所有模型参数（包括在位相互作用U、J和电子-声子耦合），通过分析U-3J与呼吸模和Jahn-Teller模的电子-声子耦合之间的竞争，研究三种竞争绝缘相。

Result: 发现三种绝缘相：(1)大U-3J时，高自旋Ni³⁺位上的局域JT畸变稳定绝缘态；(2)小U-3J时，发生电荷歧化2Ni³⁺→Ni²⁺+Ni⁴⁺，形成自旋极化电荷有序态；(3)当Ni²⁺位JT能超过Hund交换3J时，出现新的电荷和轨道有序绝缘相，两个e₉电子占据同一轨道且自旋相反。自洽计算确认了该亚稳态的稳定性。

Conclusion: 预测了一种新型非磁电荷有序RNiO₃相，其特征是JT畸变，表明RNiO₃中金属-绝缘体转变不需要磁有序的参与。

Abstract: We propose a three-dimensional multi-orbital tight-binding model for rare-earth nickelates RNiO$_3$ that treats charge, spin, orbital, and lattice degrees of freedom on equal footing. All model parameters, including the on-site interactions $U$ and $J$ and the electron-phonon (el-ph) coupling to the breathing mode, are extracted from hybrid-functional DFT calculations for the small-bandwidth nickelate LuNiO$_3$. The model describes three competing insulating phases governed by the interplay of $U{-}3J$ and el-ph coupling to the breathing and Jahn--Teller (JT) modes. For large $U{-}3J$, the insulating state is stabilized by local JT distortions on high-spin Ni$^{3+}$ sites. For smaller $U{-}3J$, the system undergoes charge disproportionation, $2\mathrm{Ni}^{3+}\rightarrow\mathrm{Ni}^{2+}+\mathrm{Ni}^{4+}$, resulting in the spin-polarized charge-ordered state observed experimentally below the Néel temperature in small-bandwidth RNiO$_3$. When the JT energy on the Ni$^{2+}$ site exceeds Hund's exchange $3J$, a distinct charge- and orbital-ordered insulating phase emerges in which the two $e_g$-electrons occupy the same orbital with opposite spin. The stability of this phase is further confirmed by self-consistent calculations within the full three-dimensional tight-binding model. This newly predicted metastable state, characterized by JT distortions in a nonmagnetic charge-ordered RNiO$_3$ phase, shows that the onset of magnetic order is not required for the metal-insulator transition in RNiO$_3$.

</details>


### [111] [Magnetic structure in the two-dimensional van der Waals ferromagnet Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09729)
*Po-Chun Chang,Sabreen Hammouda,Yung-Hsiang Tung,Yishui Zhou,Iurii Kibalin,Bachir Ouladdiaf,Chao-Hung Du,Yixi Su*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究采用化学气相输运法成功生长了二维范德华铁磁体Fe₃GaTe₂单晶，相较于传统自助熔剂法有效减少了表面杂质。通过单晶X射线和中子衍射确认其六方P6₃/mmc结构，Fe占据两个不等价位点，磁矩Feⁱ > Feⁱⁱ，易磁化轴沿c轴，居里温度约355-360 K。与Fe₃GeTe₂相比，FGaT的a轴略膨胀、c轴收缩，导致c轴方向Feⁱ-Feⁱⁱ原子间距减小，增强了Fe-Fe交换相互作用，这是其Tc显著高于FGT的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统自助熔剂法生长Fe₃GaTe₂单晶时表面杂质较多的问题，同时探究Fe₃GaTe₂相较于同体系Fe₃GeTe₂居里温度显著升高的微观物理机制，特别是晶体结构和原子间距对磁交换相互作用的影响。

Method: 采用化学气相输运法（CVT）生长高质量Fe₃GaTe₂单晶；利用单晶X射线衍射和中子衍射进行结构与磁性表征，确定原子占位、磁矩大小和易磁化轴取向。

Result: 成功生长出高质量FGaT单晶，表面杂质显著减少；确认P6₃/mmc六方结构，Feⁱ磁矩1.9(2) μB，Feⁱⁱ磁矩1.4(6) μB；易磁化轴沿c轴，Tc~355-360 K；相较于FGT，FGaT的a轴膨胀、c轴收缩，c轴方向Feⁱ-Feⁱⁱ间距减小。

Conclusion: FGaT中c轴方向Feⁱ-Feⁱⁱ原子间距的显著收缩增强了Fe-Fe交换相互作用，这是其居里温度（~355-360 K）显著高于FGT的根本原因。

Abstract: High-quality single crystals of the two-dimensional van der Waals ferromagnet Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ (FGaT) were successfully grown using the chemical vapour transport method, which effectively reduced surface impurities compared with conventional self-flux growth. Structural and magnetic characterizations were performed using single-crystal X-ray and neutron diffraction. The results confirm that FGaT crystallizes in the hexagonal $P6_3/mmc$ structure, with Fe occupying two inequivalent sites (Fe$^{i}$ and Fe$^{ii}$), where the magnetic moment of Fe$^{i}$ [1.9(2) $μ_B$] is larger than that of Fe$^{ii}$ [1.4(6) $μ_B$]. The magnetic easy axis is oriented along the $c$ axis and the Curie temperature ($T_C$) is approximately 355-360 K. Compared with Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ (FGT), FGaT exhibits a slightly expanded $a$ axis and a contracted $c$ axis, resulting in a reduction in the Fe$^{i}$-Fe$^{ii}$ interatomic distance along the $c$ axis. This pronounced contraction could strengthen the Fe$-$Fe exchange interaction, which is believed to be the key factor responsible for the significantly higher $T_C$ in FGaT relative to FGT.

</details>


### [112] [Correlation-Driven Orbital-Selective Fermiology and Superconductivity in the Bilayer Nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10101)
*Yong-Yue Zong,Shun-Li Yu,Jian-Xin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过TDVP-CPT和DMRG计算，揭示了La₃Ni₂O₇中电子关联驱动了轨道选择性重构：d_{z²}谱权重被耗尽，γ带沉入费米能级以下，α和β带打开赝能隙，强耦合下费米弧由d_{x²-y²}轨道主导。超导配对从d_{z²}主导转变为d_{x²-y²}主导的层间自旋单态配对，且保持s±结构。γ口袋的消失并非不利于超导，而是关联驱动配对通道转移的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现La₃Ni₂O₇中d_{z²}衍生的γ带可位于费米能级以下，挑战了基于DFT的三费米面图像，亟需理论研究来解释这一差异。

Method: 采用时间相关变分原理(TDVP)的团簇微扰理论(CPT)对多达16个物理位点的团簇进行计算，并结合大规模密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)计算。

Result: 电子关联导致d_{z²}谱权重耗尽、γ带下沉、α/β带出现赝能隙、强耦合下费米弧由d_{x²-y²}主导；超导关联从d_{z²}主导转为d_{x²-y²}主导的层间自旋单态配对。

Conclusion: γ口袋消失非但不抑制超导，反而标志着由层间反铁磁、Hund耦合和轨道间杂化介导的配对通道发生关联驱动的转移。

Abstract: Recent angle-resolved photoemission measurements on La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ have challenged the density-functional-theory-based picture of three Fermi surfaces by revealing that the $d_{z^2}$-derived $γ$ band can reside below the Fermi level. Motivated by this discrepancy, we investigate a realistic bilayer two-orbital Hubbard model using time-dependent variational principle (TDVP)-based cluster perturbation theory (CPT), alongside large-scale density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations. Our TDVP-CPT calculations, performed on clusters of up to 16 physical sites, reveal that electronic correlations drive a pronounced orbital-selective reconstruction of the low-energy spectrum: the $d_{z^2}$ spectral weight is progressively depleted, the $γ$ band sinks below the Fermi level, and pseudogaps open on the remaining $α$ and $β$ bands, leaving Fermi arcs dominated by the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital at strong coupling. Furthermore, large-scale DMRG calculations demonstrate that the leading superconducting correlations evolve consistently with this Fermi surface reconstruction, transitioning from $d_{z^2}$-dominated to $d_{x^2-y^2}$-dominated interlayer spin-singlet pairing while retaining an $s_{\pm}$ structure. Consequently, our results indicate that the disappearance of the $γ$ pocket is not detrimental to superconductivity; rather, it signals a correlation-driven shift of the pairing channel mediated by interlayer antiferromagnetism, Hund's coupling, and inter-orbital hybridization.

</details>


### [113] [B-H hysteresis in itinerant Feromagnetism from Chern-Simons Gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10104)
*Kenzo Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从Chern-Simons规范理论推导出多电子系统自由能中的log H项，由于H=0处的奇异性，导致对称单畴多电子系统出现一级相变和B-H磁滞回线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多电子系统在磁场下的相变行为，解释log H项如何导致非传统的一级相变和磁滞现象。

Method: 基于Chern-Simons规范理论推导多电子系统的自由能，分析其中log H项的数学奇异性及其物理后果。

Result: 发现log H项在H=0处的奇异性会导致对称单畴多电子系统出现一级相变和B-H磁滞回线。

Conclusion: 该一级相变起源于量子力学机制，与畴壁不可逆运动无关，且仅出现在对称单畴系统中。

Abstract: The log H term is derived in the free energy of many-electron system from Chern-Simons gauge theory. Owing to the singularity at $H=0$, this leads the first order transition and B-H hysteresis to many-electron systems of symmetric and single domain. This has the origin in quantum mechanics and is irrelevant to non-invertible motions of domains. This transition appears in single and symmetric domain.

</details>


### [114] [Cavity-Induced Excitonic Insulation and Non-Fermi-Liquid Behavior in Dirac Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10652)
*Yuxuan Guo,Ashida Yuto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究高阻抗超表面深亚波长腔中的二维狄拉克费米子，发现超表面支持准静电横磁模式，可通过工程化相互作用驱动狄拉克材料进入激子绝缘相（当费米子 flavors 数 N_f < 16/π 时）或非费米液体临界态（N_f > 16/π 时），并在磁场下动态提升零级朗道能级简并度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阻抗超表面腔中二维狄拉克费米子的量子电动力学效应，研究超表面支持的准静电横磁模式如何通过长程相互作用改变狄拉克材料的基态性质。

Method: 结合静态电子屏蔽与 Dyson-Schwinger 方程分析，研究工程化相互作用对狄拉克费米子基态的影响。

Result: 1) N_f < 16/π 时，相互作用驱动激子绝缘相，通过无穷级量子相变自发产生质量隙；2) N_f > 16/π 时，系统保持无能隙但进入非费米液体临界态，准粒子权重被奇异压制为零，狄拉克锥呈现非解析色散；3) 垂直磁场下，腔涨落在所有 N_f 下动态提升零级朗道能级简并度。

Conclusion: 高阻抗超表面腔是工程化关联狄拉克物质的有前景平台。

Abstract: We investigate two-dimensional Dirac fermions embedded in a deep-subwavelength cavity formed by high-impedance metasurfaces. We point out that, unlike conventional metallic boundaries, these metasurfaces support quasielectrostatic transverse-magnetic modes that mediate a long-range interaction between two-dimensional electrons. Combining static electronic screening with a Dyson-Schwinger analysis, we show that this engineered interaction can qualitatively alter the ground-state properties of Dirac materials. For a fermion flavor number $N_{f}$ below a critical value $N_{c}=16/π$, the interaction drives an excitonic insulating phase through an infinite-order quantum phase transition and spontaneously generates a mass gap. At $N_{f}>N_{c}$, the system remains gapless but enters a non-Fermi-liquid critical regime where the quasiparticle residue is singularly suppressed to zero, and the Dirac cone exhibits a nonanalytic dispersion relation. Furthermore, under a perpendicular magnetic field, the cavity fluctuations dynamically lift the zeroth Landau level degeneracy across all $N_{f}$. These results identify high-impedance metasurface cavities as promising platforms for engineering correlated Dirac matter.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Highly connected non-formal Milnor fibers via polyhedral products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09254)
*Alexander I. Suciu*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Fernández de Bobadilla的实现定理与Grbić-Linton的Massey积构造，证明了存在任意高连通且非形式的加权齐次多项式的Milnor纤维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Fernández de Bobadilla原方法中仅能得到2-连通非形式Milnor纤维的限制，即消除连通性障碍，构造具有任意高连通性的非形式Milnor纤维。

Method: 将Fernández de Bobadilla的实现定理（将加权齐次多项式的Milnor纤维等同于解析集芽的补空间）与Grbić-Linton关于moment-angle复形$\mathcal{Z}_K = \mathcal{Z}_K(D^2, S^1)$的系统性Massey积构造相结合，利用后者能构造任意$n$重及任意上同调度的非平凡Massey积的能力，消除连通性限制。

Result: 成功构造出具有任意高连通性且非形式的加权齐次多项式的Milnor纤维，突破了原方法仅能得到2-连通非形式Milnor纤维的限制。

Conclusion: 通过将实现定理与Grbić-Linton的Massey积框架结合，可以系统地构造任意高连通且非形式的Milnor纤维，彻底消除了原方法中的连通性限制。

Abstract: We show that the realization theorem of Fernández de Bobadilla, which identifies the Milnor fiber of a weighted-homogeneous polynomial with the complement of a germ of analytic set, can be combined with the systematic Massey product constructions of Grbić-Linton for moment-angle complexes $\mathcal{Z}_K = \mathcal{Z}_K(D^2, S^1)$ to produce weighted-homogeneous polynomials whose Milnor fibers are arbitrarily highly connected and non-formal. The original application of this strategy, due to Fernández de Bobadilla, used the Denham-Suciu classification of lowest-degree triple Massey products and yielded only $2$-connected non-formal Milnor fibers. The Grbić-Linton framework, which constructs non-trivial $n$-fold Massey products in $H^*(\mathcal{Z}_K;\mathbb{Z})$ for arbitrary $n$ and in arbitrary cohomological degrees, removes this connectivity restriction entirely.

</details>


### [116] [Homotopy Non-Invariance of the String Cobracket and the Failure of the Lie Bialgebra Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10273)
*Isana Sumoto*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明弦余括号不是同伦不变量，并通过计算三维透镜空间L(9;1)和L(9;4)上的弦余括号证明其不同；进一步将弦余括号与Whitehead挠率关联，并指出弦括号与弦余括号不构成Lie双代数结构，表明与Turaev余括号的类比在高维弦拓扑中失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弦余括号是否具有同伦不变性，并验证高维弦拓扑中弦括号与弦余括号能否构成Lie双代数结构，从而检验其与经典Turaev余括号类比的适用性。

Method: 借鉴Naef计算弦余积的方法（arXiv:2106.11307），将其适配到弦余括号的计算中；通过对比三维透镜空间L(9;1)和L(9;4)上的弦余括号来检验同伦不变性；同时将弦余括号与Whitehead挠率建立联系。

Result: 弦余括号不是同伦不变量；L(9;1)和L(9;4)上的弦余括号不同；弦括号与弦余括号不能赋予自由环路空间的S^1-等变同调以Lie双代数结构。

Conclusion: 弦余括号缺乏同伦不变性，且高维弦拓扑中弦括号与弦余括号不构成Lie双代数，表明其与Turaev余括号的类比在高维情形下不成立。

Abstract: We prove that the string cobracket is not a homotopy invariant. Adapting Naef's method arXiv:2106.11307 for computing the string coproduct, we show that the string cobrackets on the three-dimensional lens spaces $L(9;1)$ and $L(9;4)$ differ. We further relate the string cobracket to the Whitehead torsion, analogously to the case of the string coproduct. In addition, we show that the string bracket and the string cobracket do not endow the $S^1$-equivariant homology of the free loop space with a Lie bialgebra structure. These findings indicate that the analogy with the Turaev cobracket breaks down in higher-dimensional string topology.

</details>


### [117] [The Galois theory of $G$-spectra and the Burnside ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10280)
*Niko Naumann,Luca Pol,Maxime Ramzi*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限群G的G-谱范畴的Galois群胚等价于G的Burnside环的étale基本群胚，即该Galois群胚是代数的，并通过实现一个基于G的标记表计算后者的算法，给出了大量实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立G-谱范畴的Galois群胚与Burnside环的étale基本群胚之间的代数等价关系，从而为计算此类Galois群胚提供代数化、可计算的理论框架。

Method: 首先从理论上证明Galois群胚的代数性（等价于Burnside环的étale基本群胚），然后设计并实现一个利用有限群G的标记表来计算该étale基本群胚的算法。

Result: 成功证明了G-谱范畴的Galois群胚是代数的，并实现了从标记表计算Burnside环étale基本群胚的有效算法，提供了丰富的计算实例。

Conclusion: 该工作为稳定同伦论中的Galois理论提供了可计算的代数等价描述，并展示了通过标记表进行具体计算的可行性。

Abstract: We prove that the Galois groupoid of the category of $G$-spectra for a finite group $G$ is algebraic, i.e. equivalent to the étale fundamental groupoid of the Burnside ring of $G$. We implement an algorithm that computes the latter from the table of marks of $G$, and provide numerous examples.

</details>


### [118] [Surgery on manifold operads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10580)
*Xujia Chen,Connor Malin,Paolo Salvatore*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究“流形操作子”的协边理论，这是Fulton-MacPherson操作子的推广。通过操作子双模的树组合结构，建立了流形操作子的协边与手术理论，并应用手术构造出无穷多个与Fulton-MacPherson操作子双模协边但非同伦等价的流形操作子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将协边理论推广到流形操作子这一拓扑操作子类别，以理解不同操作子之间的更精细（比同伦等价更弱）的等价关系，并建立相应的手术理论。

Method: 利用操作子双模关联的树的精细组合结构，建立流形操作子的协边理论与手术理论。

Result: 构造了无穷多个流形操作子，它们与Fulton-MacPherson操作子存在左或右双模协边关系，但非同伦等价。

Conclusion: 流形操作子的协边理论能揭示比同伦等价更丰富的分类信息，双模协边关系是一种非平凡的操作子等价关系。

Abstract: We study cobordisms of a class of topological operads called ``manifold operads''. These operads are generalizations of the Fulton-MacPherson operad: an operad built from configurations of points in Euclidean space. Cobordism of manifold operads, along with the associated theory of surgery, depends crucially on delicate combinatorial results for trees associated to operadic bimodules. As an application of surgery, we produce infinitely many manifold operads which are left or right ``bimodule cobordant'' to, but not homotopy equivalent to the Fulton-MacPherson operad.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Semisimplicity Criteria for algebras with a Jones Basic Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08649)
*Frederick M. Goodman,Hans Wenzl*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法来证明一大类代数的半单性判据，并将其应用于Brauer代数、BMW代数和q-Brauer代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对一大类代数建立半单性判据，并推广到Brauer、BMW和q-Brauer等具体代数上。

Method: 采用一种新的证明方法，并非依赖于已有的技巧，而是独立地推导出半单性判据。

Result: 成功将该半单性判据应用于Brauer代数、BMW代数和q-Brauer代数。

Conclusion: 新方法有效且适用广泛，能够涵盖多种重要代数结构。

Abstract: We prove a semisimplicity criterion for a large class of algebras by a new method. This can be applied to Brauer, BMW, and $q$-Brauer algebras.

</details>


### [120] [BGG resolutions, Koszulity, and stratifications, part II: the Jacobi-Trudi algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09261)
*Fan Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将Jacobi-Trudi行列式公式提升为范畴化形式，通过构造称为"Jacobi-Trudi代数"的拟遗传商代数，证明其支配单模具有BGG分解，并揭示了这些代数及其Morita等价的Soergel演算具有"nil-Koszul"性质，进而通过Koszul对偶恢复BGG分解的微分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Schur函数的Jacobi-Trudi行列式公式范畴化为某种最高权现象的"影子"，揭示其背后的代数结构。

Method: 构造cyclotomic KLR代数的拟遗传商代数（Jacobi-Trudi代数），证明其nil-Koszul性，利用Koszul对偶恢复BGG分解的微分，并将BGG分解限制到$\mathbb{C}S_n$上得到Specht模的置换模分解。

Result: Jacobi-Trudi代数的支配单模存在BGG分解；这些代数及其Morita等价的Soergel演算是nil-Koszul的；Koszul对偶可恢复BGG分解的微分。

Conclusion: 该工作为范畴化中nil-Koszul代数的自然出现提供了又一例证，并进一步展示了nil-Koszul性与BGG分解之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We categorify the Jacobi-Trudi determinant formula for Schur functions as a shadow of a highest-weight phenomenon by considering certain quasi-hereditary quotients of certain cyclotomic KLR algebras, which we call ``Jacobi-Trudi algebras''. These algebras come equipped with a map from $\mathbb{C} S_n$, and we show that the dominant simple modules for these algebras admit BGG resolutions which, when restricted to $\mathbb{C} S_n$, become resolutions of Specht modules by permutation modules. We establish these BGG resolutions by showing that these Jacobi-Trudi algebras, as well as the Soergel calculi to which they are Morita equivalent, are ``nil-Koszul'', meaning that they have ``lower half subalgebras'' which are Koszul. We also show that Koszul duality with respect to this half subalgebra can be used to recover the differentials of the BGG resolutions. Hence this paper gives another example of a nil-Koszul algebra appearing naturally in categorification and gives another demonstration of the intricate connection between nil-Koszulity and BGG resolutions.

</details>


### [121] [A reciprocity theorem of Robinson-Benson-Webb for finite-dimensional symmetric algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09380)
*Shigeo Koshitani*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将有限群及其子群之间的互惠定理推广到由双模连接的两个域上有限维对称代数的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将群论中经典的 Robinson-Benson-Webb 互惠定理从有限群及其子群的语境推广到更一般的对称代数框架，从而扩展该定理的适用范畴。

Method: 通过引入连接两个对称代数的双模结构，在有限维对称代数的设定下重新表述并证明推广后的互惠关系。

Result: 成功建立了两个域上有限维对称代数之间基于双模的互惠定理，该定理是原群论版本的自然推广。

Conclusion: 该推广不仅统一了已有结果，还为对称代数间双模诱导的表示论关系提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We generalize the reciprocity theorem of G.R.~Robinson, D. Benson and P. Webb between a finite group and its subgroup to the case of finite-dimensional {\it symmetric} algebras over a field which are connected by a bimodule for the two algebras.

</details>


### [122] [Hecke monoids, their homomorphisms and parabolicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09761)
*Arkady Berenstein,Jacob Greenstein,Jian-Rong Li*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hecke幺半群之间的同态，特别是抛物同态（将抛物元映射到抛物元）和单同态。对第一类，发现了大量抛物同态；对第二类，分类了经典类型Hecke幺半群之间的局部单连通同态。作为副产品，本文还一定程度上描述了Hecke幺半群之间的所有同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛物元构成Hecke幺半群中一个相当神秘的子幺半群，研究抛物同态有助于理解其结构；此外，为分类单同态奠定基础。

Method: 通过分析Hecke幺半群的同态性质，特别关注抛物同态和局部单同态，对经典类型的Hecke幺半群进行分类研究。

Result: 发现了大量抛物同态；分类了经典类型Hecke幺半群之间所有局部单连通同态，并预期它们都是单同态；一定程度上描述了所有Hecke幺半群之间的同态。

Conclusion: 抛物同态和单同态的研究不仅揭示了Hecke幺半群的结构，还意外地帮助描述了它们之间所有可能的同态。

Abstract: We study homomorphisms of Hecke monoids, notably parabolic homomorphisms, which map parabolic elements to parabolic elements, and injective ones. The importance of the first class stems from the fact that parabolic elements form a rather mysterious submonoid of the Hecke monoid, and we found a plethora of parabolic homomorphisms.Concerning injective ones, as a first step towards their classification, we classified all locally injective connected homomorphisms between Hecke monoids of classical types and expect all of them to be injective. As a surprising byproduct of our study of parabolic and injective homomorphisms we described, to some extent, all homomorphisms between Hecke monoids.

</details>


### [123] [Middle convolution for Lie algebra representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09828)
*Kazuki Hiroe*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为李代数引入了中间卷积函子，该函子定义在特定李代数（如自由李代数、Drinfeld-Kohno李代数、超平面排列补集的完整李代数）的模范畴上。该函子是Drinfeld-Kohno李代数上Long-Moody函子的无穷小类比推广，能恢复Fuchsian系统和对数联络的中间卷积，并建立了完整李代数与局部系统之间的Riemann-Hilbert对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Dettweiler-Reiter和Haraoka等人发展的微分方程/局部系统理论中的中间卷积概念推广到李代数表示范畴，建立统一框架。

Method: 定义李代数上的中间卷积函子，通过具体验证其与Drinfeld-Kohno李代数上的Long-Moody函子、Fuchsian系统加性中间卷积、对数联络中间卷积以及Riemann-Hilbert对应的一致性来建立理论。

Result: 成功构造了李代数版本的中间卷积，证明了它与已有中间卷积概念在多种情形下的等价性或兼容性，并建立了完整李代数与局部系统之间的Riemann-Hilbert对应。

Conclusion: 李代数中间卷积为理解表示范畴中的操作提供了新工具，统一了代数表示理论与微分方程/拓扑学中的相关构造。

Abstract: This paper introduces a Lie algebra analogue of the middle convolution functor, which is defined on the category of modules over certain Lie algebras, including, as typical motivating examples, free Lie algebras, Drinfeld-Kohno Lie algebras, and the holonomy Lie algebras of complements of hyperplane arrangements. First, we demonstrate that the middle convolution for Lie algebra representations can be regarded as a natural generalization of the infinitesimal analogue of the Long-Moody functor for Drinfeld-Kohno Lie algebras. Second, we show that our middle convolution recovers the Dettweiler-Reiter additive middle convolution for Fuchsian systems on the punctured Riemann sphere as a special case. Furthermore, we show that when applied to the holonomy Lie algebra of the complement of a hyperplane arrangement, our functor is compatible with Haraoka's middle convolution for logarithmic connections on such complements. Finally, we establish a Riemann-Hilbert correspondence between the middle convolution for the holonomy Lie algebra and the middle convolution for local systems on complements of hyperplane arrangements.

</details>


### [124] [Convergence of orbital integrals on unitary groups in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10053)
*Wansu Kim,Minju Park*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在正特征非阿基米德局部域上的酉群上轨道积分的绝对收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道积分的收敛性是调和分析中的基础问题，对于建立更广泛的迹公式理论和Langlands纲领中关于酉群的某些猜想至关重要。

Method: 本文可能使用了非阿基米德局部域上代数群的相关分析技术，包括对酉群结构的具体分析、轨道积分的分解与估计等方法。

Result: 成功证明了任意正特征非阿基米德局部域上酉群的轨道积分是绝对收敛的。

Conclusion: 该结果为在正特征环境下进一步研究酉群上的调和分析以及相关数论问题提供了基础性的理论支持。

Abstract: We prove the absolute convergence of orbital integrals on a unitary group over a non-archimedean local field in any positive characteristic.

</details>


### [125] [Fractal phenomenon in $c$- and $g$-vectors of the Markov quiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10072)
*Ryota Akagi,Zhichao Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究秩3的B-不变型斜对称矩阵（包括Markov箭图）的C-和G-模式，证明修改后的c-和g-向量子模式具有线性同构，揭示G-扇的分形结构，给出基于Calkin-Wilf树的递归公式与互质整数参数化，并描述G-扇支撑补集的所有连通分支及其递归生成方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Markov型簇代数中自包含简单突变的启发，旨在理解秩3斜对称矩阵（B-不变型）的c-和g-向量的结构性质，特别是其子模式的线性同构关系与分形特征。

Method: 通过分析修改后的c-和g-向量的大类子模式，证明其线性同构性；利用类似于Calkin-Wilf树的递归关系推导显式递归公式，并以互质整数对进行参数化。

Result: 揭示了G-扇的分形结构；得到了所有修改后的c-和g-向量的显式递归公式及互质整数参数化；描述了G-扇支撑补集的所有连通分支，并证明它们由三种线性映射递归生成。

Conclusion: 该工作为B-不变型斜对称矩阵的簇代数提供了系统的c-和g-向量结构分析，展现了与Calkin-Wilf树的深刻联系，并为G-扇的几何结构提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We study the $C$- and $G$-patterns associated with rank $3$ skew-symmetrizable matrices of $B$-invariant type, including the Markov quiver. Motivated by the self-contained simple mutations in Markov-type cluster algebras, we prove that large classes of subpatterns of modified $c$- and $g$-vectors are linearly isomorphic, yielding a fractal structure of the corresponding $G$-fan. We further derive explicit recursive formulas for all modified $c$- and $g$-vectors in terms of integer pairs satisfying a recursion analogous to the Calkin-Wilf tree, which leads to a parameterization by coprime integers. As an application, we describe all connected components of the complement of the support of the $G$-fan, and show that they are generated recursively by three kinds of linear maps.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Understanding Asynchronous Inference Methods for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08168)
*Ayoub Agouzoul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种解决VLA模型异步推理中观测陈旧问题的方法（IT-RTC、TT-RTC、VLASH、A2C2），在统一代码基准下评估发现：A2C2的逐步残差修正效果最优，TT-RTC作为基于训练的方法最为鲁棒且零推理开销，IT-RTC在低延迟下有竞争力但高延迟时性能急剧下降，VLASH存在低延迟与高延迟间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人控制中具有前景，但其推理延迟导致异步执行时观测数据过时（observation staleness），影响控制性能。四种并行提出的方法（IT-RTC、TT-RTC、VLASH、A2C2）从不同角度解决此问题，但因评估条件不统一而无法公平比较，亟需系统性的对照基准研究。

Method: 构建两个统一代码库，集成四种方法并统一库与数据集版本；在Kinetix套件（使用MLPMixer策略）和LIBERO操作基准（使用SmolVLA）上进行基准测试，扫描推理延迟最高至d=20个控制步。

Result: A2C2的逐步残差修正在Kinetix上最有效，在d≤8时保持>90%解决率；在LIBERO上从d=4起表现领先。IT-RTC在低延迟下有竞争力，但在长块（H=30）和高延迟下性能急剧下降。TT-RTC作为最鲁棒的训练方法，在不同d_max选择下稳定，可泛化至训练延迟分布之外，且零推理开销。VLASH呈现由微调延迟范围[0,d_max]决定的低延迟与高延迟之间的明确权衡。

Conclusion: A2C2是最有效的方法，TT-RTC是零推理开销下最鲁棒的训练方法，IT-RTC适用范围有限，VLASH需根据延迟需求进行权衡选择。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising path to generalist robot control, but their inference latency causes observation staleness when generated actions are executed asynchronously. Several methods have been proposed concurrently to mitigate this problem: inference-time inpainting (IT-RTC), training-time delay simulation (TT-RTC), future-state-aware conditioning (VLASH), and lightweight residual correction (A2C2). Each takes a fundamentally different approach, but they have so far been evaluated independently with different codebases, base policies, and protocols. We present a systematic comparison of these four methods under controlled conditions. We develop two unified codebases that integrate all methods with harmonized library and dataset versions, and we benchmark them on the Kinetix suite with MLPMixer policies and on the LIBERO manipulation benchmark with SmolVLA, sweeping inference delays up to $d=20$ control steps. A2C2's per-step residual correction is the most effective method on Kinetix, holding above 90% solve rate up to $d=8$, and also leads on LIBERO from $d=4$ onwards. IT-RTC is competitive at low delays but degrades sharply under long chunks ($H=30$) and high delays. TT-RTC is the most robust training-based method: stable across $d_\max$ choices, generalizes beyond its training delay distribution, and adds zero inference overhead. VLASH exhibits a clear low-delay vs. high-delay trade-off governed by the fine-tuning delay range $[0,d_\max]$. Code is available at https://github.com/TheAyos/async-vla-inference

</details>


### [127] [Anatomical Landmark-Guided Deep Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Gastric Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08269)
*Haoxuan Wu,Sishen Yuan,Haitao Gao,Zhen Li,Xiuli Zuo,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于解剖标志引导的深度强化学习框架(AL-DRL)，用于自主胃部胶囊内镜导航。通过轻量级边缘-轮廓-深度融合模块，将策略建立在稳定的低维地标坐标上，弥合仿真到现实的差距。在8个患者模型仿真中实现50秒内>97%覆盖率，离体实验达到87%覆盖率，比专家手动控制减少53%操作时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线胶囊内镜(WCE)虽能无痛可视化胃肠道，但存在黏膜覆盖不完整、现有导航方法跨患者解剖结构的可迁移性差的问题，限制了其诊断潜力。

Method: 提出解剖标志引导的深度强化学习(AL-DRL)框架，包含：(1) 轻量级边缘-轮廓-深度融合模块提取低维解剖地标坐标；(2) 基于该低维状态的DRL策略；(3) 两阶段仿真到现实迁移流程，结合自适应动态规划控制器抑制物理干扰。

Result: 8个患者模型仿真中，50秒内覆盖率>97%，优于PPO、SAC、DQN基线；离体实验中平均覆盖率87%，操作时间比专家手动控制减少53%。

Conclusion: AL-DRL通过解剖标志引导和低维状态表示，有效弥合了仿真到现实的差距，实现了跨患者解剖结构可迁移的自主胃部导航，显著提升覆盖率和效率。

Abstract: Wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) enables painless visualization of the gastrointestinal tract, but its diagnostic potential is limited by incomplete mucosal coverage and poor transferability of existing navigation methods across patient anatomies. We propose a transferable, anatomical landmarkguided deep reinforcement learning (AL-DRL) framework for autonomous gastric navigation. Leveraging a lightweight edgecontour-depth fusion module, our policy operates on stable, lowdimensional landmark coordinates rather than high-dimensional video streams, effectively bridging the sim-to-real gap. In simulations across eight patient-derived models, the method achieves over 97% coverage within 50 seconds, significantly outperforming vanilla PPO, SAC, and DQN agents. A two-stage sim-to-real pipeline with an adaptive dynamic programming controller actively mitigates physical disturbances. Ex-vivo experiments demonstrate a mean coverage of 87% and a 53% reduction in procedure time compared with expert manual control.

</details>


### [128] [LE-PAVD: Learning-Enhanced Physics-Aware Vehicle Dynamics for High-Speed Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08489)
*Musabbir Ahmed Arrafi,Malik Ali,Nicholas M. Stiffler,Krishna Bhavithavya Kidambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LE-PAVD，一种融合物理先验与学习组件的混合车辆动力学模型。通过增加四个物理组件（Pacejka轮胎力、纵向载荷转移、侧向轮胎力效应、限速率执行器输入），在仿真和真实遥测数据上端到端训练。相比纯深度基线模型，在未见过的赛道上ADE降低16.1%、FDE降低20.6%、偏航率RMSE降低91.3%，同时FLOPs减少21.6%、推理速度提升约1.5倍。闭环仿真中，训练赛道圈速提升17.4%，测试赛道提升9.5%，且无赛道边界违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速自动驾驶赛车需要在操控极限下精确建模非线性车辆动力学。纯模型驱动方法存在简化假设，纯数据驱动方法缺乏物理一致性和泛化能力，因此需要一种兼具可解释性与精度的混合模型。

Method: 提出LE-PAVD混合模型，将物理先验（Pacejka轮胎力、纵向载荷转移、侧向轮胎力效应、限速率执行器输入）集成到学习框架中，通过端到端训练实现物理一致性与预测精度的平衡。

Result: 在未见过的赛道上，ADE降低16.1%、FDE降低20.6%、偏航率RMSE降低91.3%；FLOPs减少21.6%，推理速度提升约1.5倍；闭环仿真中训练赛道圈速提升17.4%，测试赛道提升9.5%，无边界违规。

Conclusion: LE-PAVD作为一种紧凑的物理引导动力学骨干模型，在提升预测精度和闭环性能的同时降低了推理成本，证明了物理先验与数据驱动方法结合的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of nonlinear vehicle dynamics is essential for high-speed autonomous racing, where controllers operate at the handling limits. Model-based methods are interpretable but rely on simplifying assumptions, while purely learned models capture nonlinearities yet often lack physical consistency and generalization. We propose LE-PAVD (Learning-Enhanced Physics-Aware Vehicle Dynamics), a hybrid model that integrates physics priors with learned components. Our architecture adds four components: load-sensitive Pacejka tire forces, longitudinal load transfer, lateral tire-force effects, and rate-limited actuator inputs. Trained end-to-end on simulation and real-world telemetry, LE-PAVD enforces physical consistency while improving state prediction accuracy. On an unseen track, LE-PAVD reduces average displacement error (ADE) by 16.1$\%$, final displacement error (FDE) by 20.6$\%$, and lowers yaw-rate root mean squared error (RMSE) by 91.3$\%$ versus a deep dynamics baseline, while using 21.6$\%$ fewer FLOPs and achieving approximately 1.50$\times$ faster inference. In closed-loop simulations, LE-PAVD consistently outperforms the baseline by achieving faster lap times by 17.4$\%$ on a training track and 9.5$\%$ on a test track, without any track boundary violations. Overall, LE-PAVD offers a compact, physics-grounded dynamics backbone that improves predictive fidelity and closed-loop performance while reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [129] [Trajectory-Consistent Flow Matching for Robust Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08511)
*Riad Ahmed,Sujosh Nag,Moniruzzaman Akash,Mostafa Hussein,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对流匹配策略中训练与推理之间的误差累积问题，提出了四种互补的改进方法：辅助整流流速度回归、多步轨迹一致性训练、速度场正则化和四阶龙格-库塔推理，并结合双视角3D点云编码器，在真实机器人任务和仿真任务上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配策略训练时优化逐点速度目标，而推理时需要对该速度场进行数值积分，这种训练-推理不匹配导致轨迹误差累积，影响机器人操作的可靠性和精度。

Method: 提出四种互补方法：(1)辅助整流流速度回归，在全时间区间提供均匀时序监督；(2)多步轨迹一致性训练，直接监督速度场在轨迹段上的积分位移，弥合训练-推理差距；(3)速度场正则化，强制时间平滑性防止振荡；(4)四阶龙格-库塔(RK4)推理，降低全局离散化误差。同时采用双视角3D点云编码器（两个独立PointNet编码器）增强空间感知。

Result: 在四个真实机器人任务（Franka机械臂和Boston Dynamics Spot）上，长时域多阶段任务成功率达70%和60%（基线为0%），精密工具放置任务达100%；三个MetaWorld仿真任务均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 轨迹级监督对于可靠策略执行至关重要，且各组件缺一不可——RK4在不平滑速度场上失效，平滑性无轨迹级监督仍会产生漂移。

Abstract: Flow matching policies learn continuous velocity fields that transport noise to actions, enabling fast deterministic inference for robot manipulation. However, standard training optimizes a pointwise velocity objective while inference requires numerical integration of that field -- a mismatch that causes compounding trajectory errors. We propose four complementary remedies: (1) auxiliary rectified flow velocity regression that provides uniform temporal supervision across the full time interval; (2) multi-step trajectory consistency training that supervises the integrated displacement of the velocity field over trajectory segments, directly closing the train-inference gap; (3) velocity field regularization that enforces temporal smoothness, preventing oscillations that destabilize integration; and (4) fourth-order Runge-Kutta (RK4) inference that reduces global discretization error by orders of magnitude over Euler methods. Critically, these components are not independently sufficient -- RK4 without a smooth velocity field fails, and smoothness without trajectory-level supervision still drifts, as our ablation study confirms. We further pair these with a dual-view 3D point cloud encoder using two independent PointNet encoders for complementary spatial perception. On four real-robot tasks across a Franka arm and a Boston Dynamics Spot, our method achieves 70% and 60% overall success on two long-horizon multi-phase tasks where both baselines score 0%, and reaches 100% on precision tool placement. Three MetaWorld simulation tasks confirm consistent improvements, validating that trajectory-level supervision is essential for reliable policy execution.

</details>


### [130] [Model-Reference Adaptive Flight Control of the 95-mg Bee++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08525)
*Francisco M. F. R. Gonçalves,Conor K. Trygstad,Néstor O. Pérez-Arancibia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于95毫克昆虫尺度扑翼飞行器Bee++的模型参考自适应控制（MRAC）架构，通过实时飞行实验验证了其在高性能位置跟踪中的适用性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对昆虫尺度扑翼飞行器（如Bee++，重95毫克）在高性能位置跟踪控制中面临的挑战，现有控制方法可能难以应对其高度非线性和动态不确定性，因此需要引入自适应控制架构来提高跟踪性能。

Method: 采用模型参考自适应控制（MRAC）架构，通过参考模型定义期望的动态行为，并利用自适应律在线调整控制器参数以补偿系统不确定性和变化，从而实现对Bee++飞行轨迹的精确跟踪。

Result: 通过实时飞行实验数据证明了所提MRAC架构的适用性、功能性以及在高性能位置跟踪中的优异表现。

Conclusion: MRAC架构能够有效提升昆虫尺度扑翼飞行器Bee++的位置跟踪性能，为微型飞行器的高精度控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce a model-reference adaptive control (MRAC) architecture for high-performance positional tracking of the Bee++, a 95-mg insect-scale flapping-wing aerial vehicle. The suitability, functionality, and high performance of the proposed approach are demonstrated using data from real-time flight experiments.

</details>


### [131] [BEACON: Cross-Domain Co-Training of Generative Robot Policies via Best-Effort Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08571)
*Antong Zhang,Han Qi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BEACON是一个基于理论指导的跨域协同训练框架，通过将跨域协同训练建模为差异感知的重要性重加权问题，联合训练扩散视觉运动策略和样本权重，在少量目标域数据下提升机器人策略的鲁棒性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人策略学习中，源域（如仿真）数据丰富但目标域（如真实世界）数据稀缺，现有方法在跨域迁移时面临域差异导致的性能下降问题，需要一种理论上有保障、实践上高效的跨域适应方法。

Method: 1) 将跨域协同训练形式化为差异感知的重要性重加权问题；2) 联合学习扩散视觉运动策略和逐样本源域权重，优化目标由目标域泛化保证指导；3) 开发可扩展的实例级差异估计器、策略与权重的随机交替更新、以及多源扩展来处理异构源域。

Result: 在sim-to-sim、sim-to-real和多源操作场景中，BEACON在鲁棒性和数据效率上优于仅用目标域、固定比例协同训练和特征对齐基线方法。

Conclusion: 即使没有显式对齐目标，BEACON通过差异感知的跨域协同训练隐式实现了特征对齐，验证了理论驱动方法在跨域机器人策略学习中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce BEACON--Best-Effort Adaptation for Cross-Domain Co-Training--a theory-driven framework for training generative robot policies with abundant source demonstrations and limited target demonstrations. BEACON casts cross-domain co-training as a discrepancy-aware importance-reweighting problem, jointly learning a diffusion-based visuomotor policy and per-sample source weights that minimize an objective informed by target-domain generalization guarantees. To make best-effort adaptation practical for high-dimensional sequence policies, we develop scalable instance-level discrepancy estimators, stochastic alternating updates for policy and weights, and a multi-source extension that balances heterogeneous source domains. Across sim-to-sim, sim-to-real, and multi-source manipulation settings, BEACON improves robustness and data efficiency over target-only, fixed-ratio co-training, and feature-alignment baselines. Importantly, even without an explicit alignment objective, BEACON achieves feature alignment as an implicit result of discrepancy-aware cross-domain co-training.

</details>


### [132] [ATAAT: Adaptive Threat-Aware Adversarial Tuning Framework against Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08612)
*Kewei Chen,Yayu Long,Shuai Li,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的后门攻击问题，揭示了传统攻击范式因"梯度干扰"（优化冲突）而失效的核心障碍，并提出自适应威胁感知对抗微调（ATAAT）框架。该框架通过"威胁-方法自适应映射"机制智能选择最优梯度解耦策略，在仅5%投毒率下实现>80%的目标攻击成功率，首次在数据投毒场景中实现隐式解耦攻击，揭示了VLA模型的关键安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型面临日益严峻的安全威胁，而传统后门攻击范式因"梯度干扰"（端到端训练中不同策略引发的优化冲突）导致攻击失败，亟需一种能够克服该障碍的新型攻击方法。

Method: 提出ATAAT框架，核心为"威胁-方法自适应映射"机制，可根据攻击者能力智能选择最优梯度解耦策略，从而解决梯度干扰问题。

Result: 在仅5%投毒率下实现>80%的稳健目标攻击成功率，具备极高隐蔽性；能高效处理复杂语义级触发器，首次在数据投毒场景中实现隐式解耦攻击。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了VLA模型中的关键安全漏洞，并为未来防御架构提供了理论和方法支持。

Abstract: Addressing the escalating security vulnerabilities in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, this study investigates backdoor attacks targeting the visual pathway. We identify a core obstacle causing the failure of traditional attack paradigms: "Gradient Interference." This phenomenon represents an optimization failure triggered by conflicting strategies during end-to-end training. To resolve this, we propose an Adaptive Threat-Aware Adversarial Tuning (ATAAT) framework. Through its core "Threat-Method Adaptive Mapping" mechanism, ATAAT intelligently selects the optimal gradient decoupling strategy based on the adversary's capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ATAAT exhibits significant advantages, achieving a highly robust Targeted Attack Success Rate (TASR > 80%) while maintaining extreme stealthiness with merely a 5% poisoning rate. It efficiently handles complex semantic-level triggers and achieves implicit decoupled attacks in data poisoning scenarios for the first time. This work reveals a critical security vulnerability in VLAs and provides theoretical and methodological support for future defense architectures.

</details>


### [133] [Geometry Guided Self-Consistency for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08638)
*Yinwei Dai,Zhuofu Chen,Lijie Yang,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KeyStone是一种推理时自一致性方法，通过并行采样K个候选动作轨迹并返回最大簇的medoid，在不增加延迟的前提下显著提升扩散模型动作生成的成功率（最高提升13.3%），无需额外训练或评判模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理AI模型通过扩散或流匹配生成动作轨迹，推理过程固有的随机性导致单轨迹采样脆弱，且这种脆弱性在多轮序列交互中不断累积放大，亟需一种无需额外模型、不增加延迟的一致性方法。

Method: 从共享模型上下文中并行采样K个候选动作块，在连续动作空间中进行聚类，返回最大簇的medoid作为最终动作。利用动作轨迹的紧凑性（推理受内存带宽限制，空闲计算资源可并行运行K条链而不增加延迟）和动作空间的几何结构化特性（欧氏距离直接反映物理相似性，无需学习判别器）实现高效选择。

Result: 在多种VLA和WAM模型上，KeyStone相比单轨迹采样在任务成功率上最高提升13.3%，延迟开销可忽略；与基于模型的选择器精度相当但无需训练成本。

Conclusion: KeyStone证明了利用动作空间的几何结构和推理时的计算冗余，可以实现免训练、零延迟代价的自一致性动作选择，为物理AI模型提供了一种简单有效的推理增强手段。

Abstract: State-of-the-art physical AI models generate a chunk of actions per inference through diffusion or flow matching, iteratively refining an initial noise sample into an action trajectory. Because this inference process is inherently stochastic, committing to a single trajectory per round is brittle, and this brittleness compounds across the many sequential rounds that comprise a complete episode. We introduce KeyStone, an inference-time self-consistency method for diffusion-based action generation that draws $K$ candidate action chunks in parallel from a shared model context, clusters them in continuous action space, and returns the medoid of the largest cluster -- no additional model required. Two properties make this practical. First, the compact nature of action trajectories makes diffusion inference memory-bandwidth bound, leaving spare compute capacity to run $K$ chains in parallel with no additional wall-clock latency. Second, unlike token or pixel spaces where distance carries no semantic meaning and selection requires a learned judge, action chunks are geometrically structured such that Euclidean distance directly reflects physical similarity, making selection principled and judge-free. Across diverse vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs), KeyStone improves task success rates by up to \textbf{13.3\%} over single-trajectory sampling with negligible latency overhead, while having on par accuracy with model-based selectors at no training cost. We open source KeyStone at https://github.com/dywsjtu/keystone.

</details>


### [134] [REAP: Reinforcement-Learning End-to-End Autonomous Parking with Gaussian Splatting Simulator for Real2Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08713)
*Changze Li,Zhe Chen,Shaoyu Chen,Lisen Mu,Yijian Li,Yuelong Yu,Qian Zhang,Qing Su,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REAP方法，结合强化学习（SAC）、行为克隆、软碰撞惩罚和3DGS仿真器，实现端到端自主泊车，尤其在极端狭窄机械车位中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段泊车方法在极端场景（如机械车位、死胡同车位）中易失败，端到端方法中模仿学习受限于数据成本和分布覆盖，强化学习则探索效率低下。

Method: 采用非对称强化学习框架下的SAC算法提升训练效率；通过行为克隆从规则规划器蒸馏能力加速收敛；引入软预测碰撞惩罚机制减少碰撞；构建Real2Sim2Real仿真器，用3DGS将真实场景数字化，再部署模型至实车跨越Sim2Real差距。

Result: 在3DGS仿真器中训练并部署至实车，REAP成功完成多种类型车位泊车，尤其验证了端到端强化学习泊车在极窄机械车位中的可行性。

Conclusion: 端到端强化学习结合仿真-真实映射方法可有效解决极端场景下的自主泊车难题。

Abstract: In recent years, autonomous parking has made significant advances, yet parking tasks still face challenges in extreme scenarios such as mechanical and dead-end parking slots, often resulting in failures. This is mainly due to traditional parking methods adopting a multistage approach, lacking the ability to optimize the parking problem as a whole. End-to-end methods enable joint optimization across perception and planning modules to eliminate the accumulation of errors, enhancing algorithm performance in extreme scenarios. Although several end-to-end parking methods use imitation or reinforcement learning, the former is limited by data cost and distribution coverage, while the latter suffers from inefficient exploration. To address these challenges, we propose a Reinforcement learning End-to-end Autonomous Parking method (REAP). REAP employs Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) within an asymmetric reinforcement learning framework to improve training efficiency and inference performance. To accelerate model convergence, we distill the capabilities of a rule-based planner into the end-to-end network through behavior cloning. We further introduce a soft predictive collision penalty mechanism to reduce collision rates by penalizing obstacle-approaching actions. To ensure that the trained reinforcement learning network can directly transfer to real-world scenarios, we have established a Real2Sim2Real simulator. In the Real2Sim step, we use 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to transform real-world scenes into digital scenes. In the Sim2Real step, we deploy the end-to-end model onto the vehicle to bridge the Sim2Real gap. Trained in the 3DGS simulator and deployed on physical vehicles, REAP successfully parks in various types of parking spaces, especially demonstrating the feasibility of end-to-end RL parking in extremely narrow mechanical slots.

</details>


### [135] [HULK: Large-scale Hierarchical Coordination under Continual and Uncertain Temporal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08722)
*Qingyuan Luo,Jie Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统在持续在线生成且具有不确定子任务数量的时序任务下的协同问题，本文提出了一种分层框架HULK，包含滚动任务分配与动态子队内协调两层结构，显著提升了大规模异构系统在不确定环境下的计算效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作假设任务是静态且预先已知的，可通过整数规划离线求解。然而实际应用中任务持续在线生成，且子任务数量不确定（如包裹数、待救援人数），离线方案需要全局重算与通信，无法适应此场景。

Method: 提出分层框架HULK，包含两个交织层：1）滚动层：在有限视界内将当前已知任务分配给子队；2）动态协调层：在在线执行过程中根据检测到的子任务进行子队内协调。两层在不同粒度和触发条件下运行。

Result: 在大规模异构系统上，针对多种时序任务和环境不确定性进行了严格验证，展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 分层协同架构通过在不同粒度上进行协调，有效解决了大规模系统在持续、不确定时序任务下的计算效率和鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems can be extremely efficient when working concurrently and collaboratively, e.g., for delivery, surveillance, search and rescue. Coordination of such teams often involves two aspects: selecting appropriate subteams for different tasks in various areas, and coordinating agents in the subteams to execute the associated subtasks. Existing work often assumes that the tasks are static and known beforehand, where an integer program can be formulated and solved offline. However, in many applications, the team-wise tasks are generated online continually by external requests, and the amount of subtasks within each task is uncertain, e.g., the number of packages to deliver or victims to rescue. The aforementioned offline solution becomes inadequate as it would require constant re-computation for the whole team and global communication to broadcast the results. Thus, this work tackles the large-scale coordination problem under continual and uncertain temporal tasks, specified as temporal logic formulas over collaborative actions. The proposed hierarchical framework, HULK, consists of two interleaved layers: the rolling assignment of currently known tasks to subteams within a certain horizon, and the dynamic coordination within a subteam given the detected subtasks during online execution. Thus, coordination is performed hierarchically at different granularities and triggering conditions, improving computational efficiency and robustness. The method is validated rigorously over large-scale heterogeneous systems under various temporal tasks and environment uncertainties.

</details>


### [136] [Latent Geometry Beyond Search: Amortizing Planning in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08732)
*Hoang Nguyen,Xiaohao Xu,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在具有平滑均匀几何结构的预训练潜空间中，将在线规划过程摊销为轻量级目标条件逆动力学模型（GC-IDM），在多个机器人控制基准上以100-130倍的速度提升达到或超越传统规划器的性能，表明结构化潜空间可将规划负担从在线优化转移到学习推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索何时学习到的表征能简化控制而不仅仅是预测：在预训练的LeWorldModel中，其潜空间经过平滑性和均匀性正则化，研究是否可以利用这种几何结构将规划过程简化为逆动力学映射，从而避免昂贵的在线搜索。

Method: 提出GC-IDM（Goal-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics Model），输入当前潜状态、目标潜状态和剩余时间步，直接输出下一动作，替代CEM等迭代规划器。利用LeWorldModel中正则化（平滑+均匀）的潜几何结构，使得局部潜空间中的运动学关系可通过逆模型学习。

Result: 在导航、接触式操作和连续控制四个基准环境的八个设置中，GC-IDM在七个设置上匹配或超过CEM，并将每步决策成本降低100-130倍。更广泛的消融实验（CEM、MPPI、iCEM、梯度方法）表明该优势不依赖于特定优化器。

Conclusion: 在线规划恢复的大部分结构已局部编码在潜表征中；足够结构化的潜空间可以将部分规划负担从在线优化转移到学习推理，为高效目标导向控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern vision-based world models can represent observations as compact yet expressive latent manifolds, but fast goal-oriented planning in these spaces remains challenging. This raises a central question: when does a learned representation simplify control, rather than merely enabling prediction? We study this question in a pretrained LeWorldModel, whose latent geometry is regularized for smoothness and uniformity. Our key insight is that, under such geometry, planning can be amortized into a latent inverse-dynamics mapping instead of requiring online search. We therefore replace iterative planning with a lightweight Goal-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics Model (GC-IDM) that maps the current latent state, goal latent state, and remaining horizon directly to the next action. Empirically, across four benchmark environments spanning navigation, contact-rich manipulation, and continuous control, our controller matches or exceeds CEM in seven of eight environment-protocol settings while reducing per-decision cost by 100-130x. A broader sweep over test-time planners (CEM, MPPI, iCEM, and gradient-based methods) shows that this result is not specific to a particular optimizer. These findings suggest that much of the structure recovered by test-time planning is already locally encoded in the latent representation. More broadly, our results indicate that sufficiently structured latent spaces can shift part of the planning burden from online optimization to learned inference.

</details>


### [137] [A Visuo-Tactile Data Collection System with Haptic Feedback for Coarse-to-Fine Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08757)
*Yeseung Kim,Nayoung Oh,Jun Park,Teetat Thamronglak,Daehyung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种视觉-触觉数据收集系统，通过直接驱动夹爪保留操作者的自然触觉反馈，并利用按钮实时标注任务的时间结构，生成用于模仿学习的多模态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习系统将操作者与接触力解耦，难以演示精细的力调制操作。

Method: 1) 直接驱动夹爪（操作者手指驱动，保留自然触觉反馈）；2) 集成视觉传感器与定制触觉阵列；3) 手柄按钮实时标注任务关键区域的时间结构；4) 融合手内力感知与时间标注生成多模态数据集。

Result: 生成的粗到细学习算法可利用结构化的任务知识，开发高质量操作策略。

Conclusion: 该系统通过保留触觉反馈和实时时间标注，有效支持接触丰富任务的模仿学习，为精细操作策略开发提供数据基础。

Abstract: We present a visuo-tactile data-collection system that generates temporally structured, contact-rich demonstrations for imitation learning. Conventional systems often decouple the operator from contact forces, which hinders the demonstration of subtle force modulation. Our system introduces a direct-drive gripper that the operator actuates with the fingers, preserving natural haptic feedback. Integrated visual sensors and custom tactile arrays capture image streams and contact geometry. A handle-mounted push button enables the operator to annotate the task's temporal structure in real time by marking task-critical regions. By fusing in-hand force perception with in-situ temporal annotation, the system produces multimodal datasets designed for coarse-to-fine learning algorithms that exploit structural task knowledge, enabling the development of high-quality manipulation policies.

</details>


### [138] [Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08758)
*Jiaxin Liu,Peng Yang,Yuping Li,Xinyue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OLSF-TRS的通用可扩展序列决策框架，将结构化组合优化与多智能体强化学习相结合，用于协调料箱处理机器人系统中的订单、料箱和机器人决策。在小规模系统中实现近最优性能（最优性差距低于3.5%），在大规模场景中相比SOTA规则方法减少8-12%甚至30%以上的料箱移动次数，同时保持实时响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电商和小批量生产的发展，料箱逐渐取代托盘成为主要的仓储容器，料箱处理机器人系统成为自动化履约中心的前沿。然而现有研究多针对特定系统的决策机制，难以泛化或迁移到其他场景，缺乏统一的决策框架。

Method: 提出OLSF-TRS框架，将结构化组合优化与多智能体强化学习相结合，协调订单、料箱和机器人三层次的序列决策过程。

Result: 小规模系统中平均最优性差距低于3.5%；大规模场景中相比SOTA规则方法减少8-12%（一种系统类型）和超过30%（另一种系统类型）的料箱移动次数，且保持实时响应能力。

Conclusion: OLSF-TRS为广泛部署的料箱处理机器人系统提供了高效、统一的订单履约决策框架，能够实现成本降低、能耗减少和吞吐量稳定性提升，适用于电商和工业物流领域。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid expansion of e-commerce and small-batch production, the size of the intralogistics load unit of finished goods, semi-finished goods and raw materials is steadily shrinking. Totes are gradually replacing pallets as the primary handling and storage container. This shift has propelled tote-handling robotic systems to the forefront of automation order fulfillment centers. The order-fulfillment decisions of tote-handling robotic systems share a common order-tote-robot sequential decision-making nature. Existing studies primarily focus on decision mechanisms tailored to particular systems, making it difficult to generalize or transfer them to other contexts. We propose an Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems (OLSF-TRS), a generalized and scalable sequential decision framework that combines structured combinatorial optimization with multi-agent reinforcement learning to coordinate order,tote, and robot decisions. On small-scale tote-handling robotic systems, OLSF-TRS achieves near-optimal performance with average optimality gaps below 3.5% across two distinct system configurations. In large-scale scenarios, OLSF-TRS consistently outperforms heuristic baselines across two different system types, reducing total tote movements by 8-12% and over 30% compared to SOTA rule-based approaches, while maintaining real-time responsiveness. These improvements translate into tangible operational benefits, including cost reduction, lower energy consumption, and enhanced throughput stability. The proposed framework delivers an efficient and unified order fulfillment decision-making framework for widely deployed tote-handling robotic systems,supporting high-quality order fulfillment in both e-commerce and industrial logistics sectors.

</details>


### [139] [ProcVLM: Learning Procedure-Grounded Progress Rewards for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08774)
*Youhe Feng,Hansen Shi,Haoyang Li,Xinlei Guo,Yang Wang,Chengyang Zhang,Jinkai Zhang,Xiaohan Zhang,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProcVLM是一种基于程序知识学习的进度感知视觉语言模型，通过推理剩余原子动作来估计任务进度，并利用60M帧的ProcCorpus-60M数据集训练，作为密集奖励信号用于长程机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型依赖轨迹级成功标签或时间插值，无法捕捉未完成步骤、停滞和失败状态，需要能反映任务程序阶段进展的密集反馈信号。

Method: 提出推理前估计范式，先推断剩余原子动作再估计进度；构建程序化监督合成流水线，通过子任务语义标注、进度预算分配和子任务内视觉变化分布生成帧级进度标签；从30个具身数据集构建ProcCorpus-60M（60M帧），训练ProcVQA用于程序感知预训练。

Result: 在ProcVQA和奖励模型基准上，ProcVLM在具身程序推理和轨迹内进度估计判别力上优于代表性基线，可作为密集奖励模型用于下游奖励引导的策略优化。

Conclusion: ProcVLM通过将进度估计锚定在程序结构和子任务内视觉变化上，实现了比时间插值更准确的任务进度估计，为长程操作任务提供了有效的密集奖励信号。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires dense feedback that reflects how a task advances through its procedural stages, not merely whether the final outcome is successful. Existing reward models often rely on trajectory-level success labels or time-based interpolation, which can conflate elapsed time with true task progress and therefore fail to capture unfinished steps, stagnation, and failure states. We present ProcVLM, a progress-aware vision-language model that learns procedure-grounded progress as a dense reward signal for manipulation. Rather than deriving progress from terminal outcomes or temporal proxies, ProcVLM grounds progress estimation in procedural structure and intra-stage visual change, and further adopts a reasoning-before-estimation paradigm that infers the remaining atomic actions before estimating task progress. Specifically, we construct this supervision by synthesizing frame-level subtask-semantic annotations, assigning progress budgets according to subtask structure, and distributing each budget based on intra-subtask visual change. To train ProcVLM at scale, we build a standardized procedural supervision synthesis pipeline and construct ProcCorpus-60M from 30 embodied datasets with 60M annotated frames, from which we derive ProcVQA for procedure-aware pretraining, with progress estimation as the central task alongside action segmentation and future planning. Experiments on ProcVQA and reward-model benchmarks show that ProcVLM improves embodied procedural reasoning and yields more discriminative trajectory-internal progress estimates than representative baselines, supporting its use as a dense reward model for downstream reward-guided policy optimization. Project page: https://procvlm.github.io/

</details>


### [140] [ElasticFlow: One-Step Physics-Consistent Policy with Elastic Time Horizons for Language-Guided Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08799)
*Kewei Chen,Yayu Long,Shuai Li,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElasticFlow提出了一种无需蒸馏、物理一致的单步策略框架，通过直接建模平均速度场重构平均场理论，并引入弹性时间视野机制解决机器人任务的时序异质性和频谱偏差，在多个基准上实现高效推理（约71Hz）并在长时域任务中超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在具身智能中表现优异，但其迭代去噪过程导致高延迟，现有加速方法又往往牺牲物理一致性，因此需要一种既高效又保持物理一致性的策略框架。

Method: 1) 通过直接建模平均速度场重构平均场理论（Mean Field Theory），实现从噪声到动作的直接单步映射；2) 提出弹性时间视野（Elastic Time Horizons）机制，通过显式编码控制粒度克服频谱偏差，实现语义指令与物理执行视野的高效对齐。

Result: 在LIBERO、CALVIN、RoboTwin等基准上，ElasticFlow实现了约71Hz的1-NFE高效推理，并在长时域任务中优于OpenVLA和π₀等SOTA方法。

Conclusion: ElasticFlow是一种高效、鲁棒且语义对齐的控制框架，在无需蒸馏的情况下实现了物理一致的单步扩散策略，具有在具身智能中应用的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have demonstrated exceptional performance in embodied AI. However, their iterative denoising process results in high latency, and existing acceleration methods often sacrifice physical consistency. To address this, we propose ElasticFlow, a distillation-free, physics-consistent one-step policy framework. We reconstruct the Mean Field Theory by directly modeling the average velocity field, enabling a direct single-step mapping from noise to action. Addressing the Temporal Heterogeneity of robotic tasks, we introduce the Elastic Time Horizons mechanism. This mechanism effectively overcomes Spectral Bias by explicitly encoding control granularity, achieving efficient alignment between semantic instructions and physical execution horizons. Experiments on benchmarks such as LIBERO, CALVIN, and RoboTwin demonstrate that ElasticFlow achieves efficient 1-NFE inference (approximately 71Hz). Furthermore, it outperforms state-of-the-art methods, including OpenVLA and $π_0$, on long-horizon tasks, highlighting its potential for efficient, robust, and semantically aligned control.

</details>


### [141] [Constraint-Aware Diffusion Priors for High-Fidelity and Versatile Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08804)
*Jianhui Chen,Ruixin Zhan,Liu Liu,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Diff-CAST框架，用扩散模型替代传统GAN判别器解决模式崩塌问题，结合对称增强命令调节和约束强化学习，实现四足机器人鲁棒、安全、漂移-free的多技能运动控制与数据规模化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习结合模仿学习的框架在处理大规模、多源数据集时存在三大瓶颈：1)传统GAN判别器易出现模式崩塌，无法捕获非 curated 数据集的多样运动分布；2)现有运动学先验面临分布外跟踪冲突，导致复杂机动中严重的意外航向漂移；3)将无约束先验部署到物理硬件时忽略执行器动力学，存在关键安全风险。

Method: 提出Diff-CAST框架，核心包含三个组件：1)利用扩散模型的多模态分布建模能力替代传统GAN判别器作为风格奖励，实现鲁棒的数据规模化；2)对称增强命令调节(SACC)实现无漂移跟踪；3)约束强化学习确保硬件安全性。整体构成完整的Sim2Real架构。

Result: 在四足机器人上的实验表明，Diff-CAST有效缓解了模式崩塌，实现了不同技能之间的无缝过渡，并确保了鲁棒、符合硬件约束的运动控制。

Conclusion: Diff-CAST通过扩散模型驱动的风格奖励、对称命令调节和约束强化学习的协同设计，为四足机器人大规模异构数据下的运动学习提供了更稳定、更安全、更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning combined with imitation learning has significantly advanced biomimetic quadrupedal locomotion. However, scaling these frameworks to massive, multi-source datasets exposes fundamental bottlenecks. First, traditional GAN-based discriminators are prone to mode collapse, struggling to capture diverse motion distributions from uncurated datasets. Second, existing kinematic priors suffer from out-of-distribution (OOD) tracking conflicts, leading to severe unintended heading drifts during complex maneuvers. Furthermore, deploying unconstrained priors to physical hardware poses critical safety risks by disregarding actuator dynamics. To overcome these challenges, we propose Diff-CAST (Diffusion-guided Constraint-Aware Symmetric Tracking), a novel motion prior framework leveraging the multi-modal distribution modeling capabilities of diffusion models for stylistic rewards. Diff-CAST effectively replaces traditional GAN discriminators, unlocking robust data scaling on heterogeneous collections. To ensure high-fidelity intent execution and reliable real-world deployment, we introduce a comprehensive Sim2Re architecture integrating Symmetric Augmented Command Conditioning (SACC) for drift-free tracking, and Constrained RL for hardware safety. Experiments on a quadruped demonstrate that Diff-CAST mitigates mode collapse, enables seamless transitions between diverse skills, and ensures robust, hardware-compliant locomotion.

</details>


### [142] [AssemPlanner: A Multi-Agent Based Task Planning Framework for Flexible Assembly System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08831)
*Chenhao Zhang,Chaoran Zhang,Zhaobo Xu,Yongbo Yang,Pingfa Feng,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体的柔性装配系统任务规划框架AssemPlanner，通过自然语言输入自动生成可执行的生产操作序列，利用ReAct驱动的SchedAgent结合多智能体反馈自适应调整动作，无需多专家耗时配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性装配系统的任务规划方法需要多位专家耗时配置才能建立新产品的生产线，效率低下。

Method: 提出AssemPlanner框架，包含SchedAgent、KnowledgeAgent、LineBalanceAgent和场景图；SchedAgent作为核心推理引擎，采用ReAct机制，通过多智能体反馈自适应调整动作。

Result: 该框架能够将自然语言描述的任务自动转换为可执行的生产操作序列，并自主解决复杂的工业过程约束。

Conclusion: AssemPlanner通过多智能体协作和ReAct推理机制，有效降低了柔性装配系统配置的时间和专家依赖，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: In flexible assembly systems, existing task planning methods require a time-consuming configuration process by multiple experts to establish a production line for a new product. To address this challenge, we propose a multi-agent based task planning framework for flexible assembly systems, denoted as AssemPlanner. It takes tasks described in natural language as input, which are then converted into actionable sequential production operations. It comprises several specialized agents, including SchedAgent , KnowledgeAgent, LineBalanceAgent, and a scene graph. Within the proposed framework, SchedAgent serves as the central reasoning engine. Departing from traditional static pipelines, AssemPlanner utilizes a ReAct-based SchedAgent to adaptively adjust actions via multi-agent feedback.
